# 哈萨克斯坦国家统计局：2021年人口普查数据

PopulaƟon of the Republic of Kazakhstan Astana 2023 www.stat.gov.kz Agency of the Republic of Kazakhstan for Strategic Planning and reforms National Bureau of Statistics Population of the Republic of Kazakhstan Results of the National Population Census of 2021 in the Republic of Kazakhstan Astana 2023 Population of the Republic of Kazakhstan 1 volume / in English / 227 pages Agency for Strategic Planning and Reforms of the Republic of Kazakhstan Bureau of National Statistics The collection is available on the Internet resource of the Bureau of National Statistics of the Agency for Strategic Planning and Reforms of the Republic of Kazakhstan www.stat.gov.kz .
Address: 8 Mangilik el Avenue, Astana, 010000, Republic of Kazakhstan House of Ministries, 4th entrance Agency for Strategic Planning and Reforms of the Republic of Kazakhstan Bureau of National Statistics Unified contact center: 1446 Email address: e.stat@aspire.gov.kz Online resource: http://stat.gov.kz Symbols: - - no phenomenon 0.0 is a minor value In individual cases, the slight differences between the sum of the sum and the sum of the inclusions are explained by the rounding of the data.
© Agency of the Republic of Kazakhstan for Strategic Planning and reforms Bureau of National Statistics Population of the Republic of Kazakhstan 3 www.stat.gov.kz Preface The statistical collection «Population of the Republic of Kazakhstan» is published in two volumes. The first volume contains data from the 2009 and 2021 censuses, population changes over the inter-census periods. It contains data on the administrative-territorial division, information on the population, its location on the territory of Kazakhstan, and population changes. The statistical collection is intended for senior management personnel, employees of management bodies and financial and economic services of enterprises, organizations, scientific, entrepreneurial and banking circles, teaching staff, graduate students and students of economic universities, for a wide range of readers who need to receive objective and complete information on the results of the national population census 2021 in the Republic of Kazakhstan.
The statistical collection was prepared by specialists of the Department of Agricultural Statistics and National Censuses of the Bureau of National Statistics of the Agency for Strategic Planning and Reforms of the Republic of Kazakhstan and the Republican State Enterprise on the right of economic management «Information and Computing Center of the Bureau of National Statistics of the Agency for Strategic Planning and Reforms of the Republic of Kazakhstan».
